### File 3: `ui/widgets/chord_progression_editor.py`

```python
"""
Sonorum - Chord Progression Editor
Widget for editing chord progressions within a section
"""

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGridLayout,
    QPushButton, QComboBox, QLabel, QGroupBox, QScrollArea
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal

from core.project import ChordChange


class ChordProgressionEditor(QWidget):
    """Widget for editing chord progressions"""
    
    chord_progression_changed = Signal()
    
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.current_section = None
        self.chord_combos = []
        
        self.setup_ui()
    
    def setup_ui(self):
        """Setup UI"""
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.setLayout(layout)
        
        # Group box
        self.group = QGroupBox("Chord Progression")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # Scroll area for chord grid
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        
        # Container for chord grid
        self.chord_container = QWidget()
        self.chord_grid = QGridLayout()
        self.chord_container.setLayout(self.chord_grid)
        scroll.setWidget(self.chord_container)
        
        group_layout.addWidget(scroll)
        
        # Buttons
        button_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        self.auto_fill_btn = QPushButton("Auto-fill Diatonic")
        self.auto_fill_btn.clicked.connect(self.auto_fill_diatonic)
        self.auto_fill_btn.setToolTip("Fill with common chord progression in 
current key")
        button_layout.addWidget(self.auto_fill_btn)
        
        self.copy_pattern_btn = QPushButton("Copy Pattern")
        self.copy_pattern_btn.clicked.connect(self.copy_pattern)
        self.copy_pattern_btn.setToolTip("Copy first 4 bars to remaining bars")
        button_layout.addWidget(self.copy_pattern_btn)
        
        self.clear_btn = QPushButton("Clear All")
        self.clear_btn.clicked.connect(self.clear_all)
        button_layout.addWidget(self.clear_btn)
        
        button_layout.addStretch()
        
        group_layout.addLayout(button_layout)
        
        self.group.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(self.group)
    
    def load_section(self, section):
        """Load section and create chord grid"""
        self.current_section = section
        self.create_chord_grid(section.duration)
        self.load_chord_progression(section.chord_progression)
        self.group.setTitle(f"Chord Progression - {section.name}")
    
    def create_chord_grid(self, num_bars):
        """Create grid of chord selectors"""
        # Clear existing
        while self.chord_grid.count():
            item = self.chord_grid.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()
        
        self.chord_combos = []
        
        # Common chords (expandable later)
        chord_choices = [
            "",  # Empty
            "C", "Dm", "Em", "F", "G", "Am", "Bdim",  # C major
            "D", "Em", "F#m", "G", "A", "Bm", "C#dim",  # D major
            "E", "F#m", "G#m", "A", "B", "C#m", "D#dim",  # E major
            "F", "Gm", "Am", "Bb", "C", "Dm", "Edim",  # F major
            "G", "Am", "Bm", "C", "D", "Em", "F#dim",  # G major
            "A", "Bm", "C#m", "D", "E", "F#m", "G#dim",  # A major
            "Bb", "Cm", "Dm", "Eb", "F", "Gm", "Adim",  # Bb major
            # Add more keys and qualities as needed
            "C7", "D7", "E7", "F7", "G7", "A7", "B7",
            "Cmaj7", "Dmaj7", "Emaj7", "Fmaj7", "Gmaj7", "Amaj7", "Bmaj7",
            "Cm7", "Dm7", "Em7", "Fm7", "Gm7", "Am7", "Bm7"
        ]
        
        # Create grid (4 columns)
        cols = 4
        for bar in range(num_bars):
            row = bar // cols
            col = bar % cols
            
            # Bar label
            label = QLabel(f"Bar {bar + 1}:")
            self.chord_grid.addWidget(label, row * 2, col * 2)
            
            # Chord selector
            combo = QComboBox()
            combo.addItems(chord_choices)
            combo.setEditable(True)  # Allow custom chords
            combo.currentTextChanged.connect(self.on_chord_changed)
            combo.setProperty('bar', bar + 1)  # Store bar number
            self.chord_grid.addWidget(combo, row * 2 + 1, col * 2)
            
            self.chord_combos.append(combo)
    
    def load_chord_progression(self, chord_progression):
        """Load existing chord progression into grid"""
        for chord_change in chord_progression:
            bar_index = chord_change.bar - 1  # Convert to 0-based
            if 0 <= bar_index < len(self.chord_combos):
                combo = self.chord_combos[bar_index]
                
                # Set chord (add if not in list)
                index = combo.findText(chord_change.chord)
                if index >= 0:
                    combo.setCurrentIndex(index)
                else:
                    combo.setCurrentText(chord_change.chord)
    
    def on_chord_changed(self, chord):
        """Handle chord change"""
        if not self.current_section:
            return
        
        sender = self.sender()
        bar = sender.property('bar')
        
        # Update section's chord progression
        # Remove existing change for this bar
        self.current_section.chord_progression = [
            c for c in self.current_section.chord_progression if c.bar != bar
        ]
        
        # Add new change if chord is not empty
        if chord.strip():
            self.current_section.chord_progression.append(
                ChordChange(bar=bar, chord=chord)
            )
        
        # Sort by bar number
        self.current_section.chord_progression.sort(key=lambda c: c.bar)
        
        # Emit signal
        self.chord_progression_changed.emit()
    
    def auto_fill_diatonic(self):
        """Auto-fill with common diatonic progression"""
        if not self.current_section:
            return
        
        # Simple I-vi-IV-V progression
        # This should be smarter and use actual key from project
        # For now, just a placeholder pattern
        pattern = ["C", "Am", "F", "G"]
        
        for i, combo in enumerate(self.chord_combos):
            chord = pattern[i % len(pattern)]
            combo.setCurrentText(chord)
    
    def copy_pattern(self):
        """Copy first 4 bars to rest"""
        if len(self.chord_combos) < 4:
            return
        
        # Get first 4 chords
        pattern = [combo.currentText() for combo in self.chord_combos[:4]]
        
        # Apply to remaining bars
        for i in range(4, len(self.chord_combos)):
            chord = pattern[i % 4]
            self.chord_combos[i].setCurrentText(chord)
    
    def clear_all(self):
        """Clear all chords"""
        for combo in self.chord_combos:
            combo.setCurrentIndex(0)  # Set to empty
    
    def clear(self):
        """Clear widget (no section selected)"""
        self.current_section = None
        
        # Clear grid
        while self.chord_grid.count():
            item = self.chord_grid.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()
        
        self.chord_combos = []
        self.group.setTitle("Chord Progression")
```

```python
"""
Sonorum - Main Window
Central application window with tab navigation
"""

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QMainWindow, QTabWidget, QMessageBox, QFileDialog
)
from PySide6.QtGui import QAction, QKeySequence
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal

from core.project import Project, create_new_project, create_example_project
from ui.pages.song_core import SongCorePage
from ui.pages.master_composition import MasterCompositionPage


class SonorumMainWindow(QMainWindow):
    """Main application window"""
    
    # Signals
    project_changed = Signal()
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        
        # Current project
        self.current_project = None
        self.current_file_path = None
        
        # Setup UI
        self.setup_ui()
        self.create_menus()
        self.create_toolbar()
        
        # Load or create project
        self.new_project()
    
    def setup_ui(self):
        """Setup main user interface"""
        self.setWindowTitle("Sonorum - Band Songwriting Tool")
        self.setGeometry(100, 100, 1400, 900)
        
        # Central tab widget
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.tabs.setTabPosition(QTabWidget.North)
        self.tabs.setMovable(False)
        self.setCentralWidget(self.tabs)
        
        # Create pages
        self.song_core_page = SongCorePage(self)
        self.master_comp_page = MasterCompositionPage(self)
        
        # Add tabs
        self.tabs.addTab(self.song_core_page, "Song Core")
        self.tabs.addTab(self.master_comp_page, "Master Composition")
        # More tabs will be added dynamically based on instruments
    
    def create_menus(self):
        """Create menu bar"""
        menubar = self.menuBar()
        
        # File menu
        file_menu = menubar.addMenu('&File')
        
        # New project
        new_action = QAction('&New Project', self)
        new_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.New)
        new_action.setStatusTip('Create a new project')
        new_action.triggered.connect(self.new_project)
        file_menu.addAction(new_action)
        
        # Open project
        open_action = QAction('&Open...', self)
        open_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.Open)
        open_action.setStatusTip('Open an existing project')
        open_action.triggered.connect(self.open_project)
        file_menu.addAction(open_action)
        
        file_menu.addSeparator()
        
        # Save project
        save_action = QAction('&Save', self)
        save_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.Save)
        save_action.setStatusTip('Save current project')
        save_action.triggered.connect(self.save_project)
        file_menu.addAction(save_action)
        
        # Save as
        save_as_action = QAction('Save &As...', self)
        save_as_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.SaveAs)
        save_as_action.setStatusTip('Save project as new file')
        save_as_action.triggered.connect(self.save_project_as)
        file_menu.addAction(save_as_action)
        
        file_menu.addSeparator()
        
        # Export
        export_menu = file_menu.addMenu('Export')
        
        export_pdf_action = QAction('Export as PDF...', self)
        export_pdf_action.setStatusTip('Export all parts as PDF')
        export_pdf_action.triggered.connect(self.export_pdf)
        export_menu.addAction(export_pdf_action)
        
        file_menu.addSeparator()
        
        # Quit
        quit_action = QAction('&Quit', self)
        quit_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.Quit)
        quit_action.setStatusTip('Exit application')
        quit_action.triggered.connect(self.close)
        file_menu.addAction(quit_action)
        
        # Edit menu
        edit_menu = menubar.addMenu('&Edit')
        
        undo_action = QAction('&Undo', self)
        undo_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.Undo)
        undo_action.triggered.connect(self.undo)
        edit_menu.addAction(undo_action)
        
        redo_action = QAction('&Redo', self)
        redo_action.setShortcut(QKeySequence.StandardKey.Redo)
        redo_action.triggered.connect(self.redo)
        edit_menu.addAction(redo_action)
        
        # Help menu
        help_menu = menubar.addMenu('&Help')
        
        about_action = QAction('&About', self)
        about_action.triggered.connect(self.show_about)
        help_menu.addAction(about_action)
    
    def create_toolbar(self):
        """Create toolbar"""
        toolbar = self.addToolBar('Main')
        toolbar.setMovable(False)
        
        # Add common actions
        # (Will add more as we build features)
    
    def new_project(self):
        """Create a new project"""
        # TODO: Check if current project has unsaved changes
        
        self.current_project = create_example_project()  # Use example for now
        self.current_file_path = None
        self.update_window_title()
        self.project_changed.emit()
        
        # Update pages
        self.song_core_page.load_project(self.current_project)
        self.master_comp_page.load_project(self.current_project)
    
    def open_project(self):
        """Open an existing project"""
        filename, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self,
            "Open Project",
            "",
            "Sonorum Files (*.sonorum *.son);;All Files (*)"
        )
        
        if filename:
            try:
                with open(filename, 'r') as f:
                    json_data = f.read()
                    self.current_project = Project.from_json(json_data)
                    self.current_file_path = filename
                    self.update_window_title()
                    self.project_changed.emit()
                    
                    # Update UI
                    self.song_core_page.load_project(self.current_project)
                    self.master_comp_page.load_project(self.current_project)
                    
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(
                    self,
                    "Error Opening Project",
                    f"Could not open project file:\n{str(e)}"
                )
    
    def save_project(self):
        """Save current project"""
        if self.current_file_path:
            self._save_to_file(self.current_file_path)
        else:
            self.save_project_as()
    
    def save_project_as(self):
        """Save project as new file"""
        filename, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self,
            "Save Project As",
            "",
            "Sonorum Files (*.sonorum);;All Files (*)"
        )
        
        if filename:
            # Add extension if not present
            if not filename.endswith('.sonorum'):
                filename += '.sonorum'
            
            self._save_to_file(filename)
    
    def _save_to_file(self, filename: str):
        """Internal save method"""
        try:
            with open(filename, 'w') as f:
                f.write(self.current_project.to_json())
            
            self.current_file_path = filename
            self.update_window_title()
            
            # Show success message in status bar
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Saved: {filename}", 3000)
            
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(
                self,
                "Error Saving Project",
                f"Could not save project file:\n{str(e)}"
            )
    
    def export_pdf(self):
        """Export project as PDF"""
        # TODO: Implement PDF export
        QMessageBox.information(
            self,
            "Export PDF",
            "PDF export will be implemented soon!"
        )
    
    def undo(self):
        """Undo last action"""
        # TODO: Implement undo
        pass
    
    def redo(self):
        """Redo last undone action"""
        # TODO: Implement redo
        pass
    
    def update_window_title(self):
        """Update window title with project name"""
        title = "Sonorum"
        
        if self.current_project:
            title += f" - {self.current_project.metadata.title}"
        
        if self.current_file_path:
            title += f" [{self.current_file_path}]"
        else:
            title += " [Unsaved]"
        
        self.setWindowTitle(title)
    
    def show_about(self):
        """Show about dialog"""
        QMessageBox.about(
            self,
            "About Sonorum",
            "<h2>Sonorum v2.0</h2>"
            "<p>Professional band songwriting tool for KDE Plasma</p>"
            "<p>Built with Python and Qt6</p>"
            "<p>© 2026 LordFingers</p>"
        )
    
    def closeEvent(self, event):
        """Handle window close"""
        # TODO: Check for unsaved changes
        event.accept()
```

## **HIGH PRIORITY** (Core Gaps)

### 1. **Undo/Redo Stack** ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐
Right now if you make a mistake, you're screwed. This is **critical** for any 
editor.
```python
# Add to main_window.py
self.undo_stack = QUndoStack()
# Wire up to all edit operations
```

### 2. **Copy/Paste Measures** ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐
You wrote a killer riff? Want to repeat it? Currently can't. Super common use 
case.

### 3. **Unsaved Changes Warning** ⭐⭐⭐⭐
Close without saving? Lose all your work. Need that "Save changes?" dialog.

### 4. **Keyboard Shortcuts for Tab Entry** ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐
Clicking cells is slow. Real workflow:
- Arrow keys to move
- Number keys (0-9) to enter frets
- Delete to clear
- Space to advance

### 5. **Metronome/Click Track** ⭐⭐⭐⭐
Can't feel the tempo by just looking at 120 BPM. Need to hear it.

---

## **MEDIUM PRIORITY** (Quality of Life)

### 6. **Chord Library/Favorites**
Save common progressions: "I-V-vi-IV", "ii-V-I", etc.

### 7. **Scale Finder on Fretboard**
Toggle button: "Show Dm Pentatonic", "Show C Mixolydian"

### 8. **Note Duration Selector**
Currently notes are all same duration. Need: whole, half, quarter, eighth, 
sixteenth notes.

### 9. **Technique Symbols**
- Palm mute (already has UI, needs implementation)
- Slides (/, \)
- Hammer-on/Pull-off (h, p)
- Bends (b)
- Vibrato (~)

### 10. **Measure Number Display**
Show which measure you're on in the tab grid header.

### 11. **Section Jump Buttons**
Quick navigation: [← Prev Section] [Next Section →]

### 12. **Auto-Save**
Save draft every 60 seconds to prevent data loss.

---

## **NICE TO HAVE** (Polish)

### 13. **MIDI Playback** 🎵
Hear what you wrote! Export to MIDI or play through speakers.

### 14. **Song Tempo Map**
Tempo changes within song (ritardando, accelerando).

### 15. **Alternate Tuning Presets**
Quick-switch: "Drop C", "Drop D", "Open G", etc. per section.

### 16. **Measure Repeat Signs**
Instead of copying, just show "%"  repeat symbols.

### 17. **Comments/Notes**
Add text notes: "Play this quieter", "Let ring", etc.

### 18. **Colored Sections**
Visual coding: Verse = blue, Chorus = red, etc.

### 19. **Export Individual Parts**
Export "Lead Guitar Only.pdf", "Bass Only.pdf", etc.

### 20. **Import From Guitar Pro**
Lots of existing tabs in .gp5 format.

### 21. **Multi-Track Recording**

"What chord comes next?" based on music theory.

- Warns on close

### ✅ **3. KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS FOR TAB**
- **0-9** = Insert fret
- **Arrow keys** = Navigate
- **Space** = Advance
- **Delete/Backspace** = Clear
- **10x faster than clicking!**

### ✅ **4. COPY/PASTE MEASURES**
- **Ctrl+C** = Copy measure
- **Ctrl+V** = Paste measure
- Works across sections
- Status bar feedback

### BONUS: **Section Navigation**
- **Ctrl+Up/Down** = Jump sections

---

## **Keyboard Reference:**

```
TAB ENTRY:       0-9 (frets), Arrows (navigate), Space (advance), Delete (clear)
EDITING:         Ctrl+Z (undo), Ctrl+Shift+Z (redo), Ctrl+C/V (copy/paste)
NAVIGATION:      Ctrl+Up/Down (sections)
FILE:            Ctrl+S (save), Ctrl+N (new), Ctrl+O (open)
```

## ✅ IMPORT ERROR FIXED!

**Problem**: `sonorum.py` was trying to import `MainWindow` but the class is 
actually named `SonorumMainWindow`

**Solution**: Changed the import from:
```python
from ui.main_window import MainWindow
window = MainWindow()
```

To:
```python
from ui.main_window import SonorumMainWindow
window = SonorumMainWindow()
```

**Escape Sequence Warnings**: These are just warnings from `\n` in strings - 
they're harmless and won't prevent the app from running. They're coming from 
your Python 3.12+ which is stricter about escape sequences.

Claude:  ## 🤔 LET ME EXPLAIN MY THINKING

---

## **WHAT I WAS THINKING:**

### Approach 1: Mode Toggle System
Add a "Composition Mode" selector to each section or instrument:
- **Sketch Mode** → Hide detailed entry, show only chord charts + notes
- **Score Mode** → Full tab grid, every note specified
- Toggle changes what UI elements are visible/required

### Approach 2: Different Export Formats
- **Sketch** → PDF with just chord charts, key, tempo, "feel" notes
- **Score** → PDF with full tablature, every measure filled

### Approach 3: Section-Level Completeness Tracking
- Mark section as "Sketch" or "Complete"
- Sketch = chords + notes like "funky rhythm, staccato"
- Complete = every beat has a note

---

## **BUT HERE'S MY QUESTION:**

Is this about:

**A) Different tools/interfaces?**
- Sketch mode = simpler UI (just chord progression builder + text notes)
- Score mode = full tab editor (what we have now)

**B) Different mindsets for the same tools?**
- Some sections you just write "Em - heavy, palm mute, eighth notes"
- Other sections you meticulously compose every single note
- Both use the same tab editor, just filled in differently

**C) Different output formats?**
- Same project, but export can be "lead sheet" style OR "full score" style

**D) Section-level metadata?**
- Tag sections as "Sketch - improvise around this" vs "Score - play exactly 
this"
- Affects how it's presented to band members

---

## **WHAT I THINK YOU'RE DESCRIBING:**

The **same project** can have:
- **Verse**: "Just play G-Em-C-D, keep it sparse" (sketch)
- **Chorus**: Every note of the riff is written out, exact timing (score)
- **Solo section**: "A minor pentatonic, 8 bars, go wild" (sketch)
- **Outro**: Precisely notated unison riff, all instruments (score)

So it's more about **flexibility in how complete each section needs to be**, 
not necessarily two different modes?

---

## **WHAT WOULD BE USEFUL?**

Tell me what you envision:

1. **Can the same song have sketch sections AND score sections?**
2. **Is it per-section or per-instrument?** (Jack writes everything out, Sarah 
just gets chord charts?)
3. **Does the UI need to change** or just what you choose to fill in?
4. **For export**: Does sketch section PDF look different than score section 
PDF?

- ✅/❌ Section headers (name, chords, notes, tempo changes)
- ✅/❌ Reference charts (chord diagrams, scale charts, fretboard)
- **Mood/Feel field** - "Dark and brooding", "Uplifting"
- Layout options (page size, margins, font)

**Right Panel** - Live preview:
- See exactly how it will look
- Updates as you toggle options
- Preview header boxes, section boxes, charts

**Bottom** - Export actions:
- 📄 Export PDF (full score)
- 📑 Export Individual Parts (per musician)

---

## **HOW IT SUPPORTS BOTH STYLES:**

### Sketch Approach:
```
✅ Show chords
✅ Show section notes ("Funky rhythm, let the band groove")
✅ Include chord diagrams
❌ Hide empty measures
Mood: "Laid back, groovy"

→ Exports lead sheet style
→ Band develops it
```

### Score Approach:
```
✅ Show everything
✅ Tempo changes marked
✅ Detailed section notes
✅ Show empty measures (even silence is scored)
Mood: "Precise orchestration - play exactly as written"

→ Exports full score
→ Band follows exactly
```

### Mixed (Same Song):
- Verse: Just chords (sketch)
- Chorus: Every note (score)
- Solo: "Am pentatonic - improvise" (sketch)
- Outro: Exact unison riff (score)

**Export Composer shows both in same document!**

LordFingers: DEBUG: song_core calling refresh_instrument_tabs DEBUG: parent type: <class 
'PySide6.QtWidgets.QStackedWidget'> DEBUG: has refresh method: False DEBUG:

Claude:  ## 🎯 FOUND IT!!!

The parent is **QStackedWidget**, not the main window! That's why it's not 
working!

---

## **THE PROBLEM:**

`self.parent()` returns the **tab widget**, not the main window.

Song Core is inside a tab, so:
- Song Core → parent = Tab Widget
- Tab Widget → parent = Main Window

We need to go **two levels up**!

---

Claude:  ## 📜 SONORUM - MISSION STATEMENT & COMPLETE STATUS

---

# **MISSION STATEMENT**

## What is Sonorum?

**Sonorum is a comprehensive band-oriented songwriting and composition tool 
that bridges the gap between sketch and score.**

### Core Philosophy:

**"Vapor Wave Breadcrumbs"** - Theory guides without restricting creativity. 
Sonorum provides intelligent suggestions and visual hints that pull you in the 
right direction, but never blocks your path.

### Who Is It For?

1. **Solo Composers** writing multi-instrument arrangements
2. **Bands** collaborating on song structure and parts
3. **Musicians** who think in tablature, not traditional notation
4. **Songwriters** who need flexibility between loose guidelines and precise 
orchestration

### What Makes It Different?

- **Tab-First Philosophy** - Faster and more intuitive than traditional notation
- **Dual Workflow Support** - Work in "sketch mode" (chord charts + feel notes) 
OR "score mode" (every note specified) OR mix both in the same song
- **Multi-Instrument Architecture** - Dedicated editors for Guitar/Bass, Drums, 
and Vocals - no hacks
- **Theory Integration** - Built-in theory assistance that teaches while you 
compose
- **Export as Composition** - The export process itself is a creative tool for 
arranging the final document

### What It Does:

- **Organize** song structure (sections, chord progressions, tempo maps)
- **Compose** individual instrument parts with proper notation
- **Integrate** music theory as visual guides and intelligent suggestions
- **Educate** through interactive theory sandbox and comprehensive help
- **Export** publication-ready PDFs with customizable layouts
- **Preserve** creative intent through flexible sketch-to-score spectrum

---

# **COMPLETE FEATURE INVENTORY**

## ✅ FULLY IMPLEMENTED (Working)

### **1. Project Management**
- ✅ Create/save/load projects (.sonorum JSON format)
- ✅ Undo/redo system
- ✅ Auto-save every 2 minutes
- ✅ Project metadata (title, composer, key, tempo, time signature)
- ✅ Window title shows current file

### **2. Song Core**
- ✅ Song metadata editing
- ✅ Instrument management (add/edit/remove)
- ✅ Instrument list display with icons
- ✅ Multiple tuning presets (Standard, Drop D, Drop C, etc.)
- ✅ Custom tuning support
- ✅ Support for: Electric Guitar, Acoustic Guitar, Bass, Drums, Vocals, Keys

### **3. Master Composition**
- ✅ Section management (add/edit/delete/reorder)
- ✅ Section types (Intro, Verse, Chorus, Bridge, Solo, Outro, etc.)
- ✅ Section duration (bars)
- ✅ Chord progression editor (per section)
- ✅ Key override per section (modulation support)
- ✅ Tempo override per section
- ✅ Section color coding
- ✅ Section notes (performance instructions)
- ✅ Tempo changes within sections (accelerando, ritardando)
- ✅ Section list with drag-to-reorder
- ✅ Section deletion with confirmation

### **4. Guitar/Bass Tab Editor**
- ✅ Full tablature grid (32 subdivisions per bar)
- ✅ Fret number entry (0-22)
- ✅ Arrow key navigation
- ✅ Multi-string selection mode (power chords, etc.)
- ✅ Quick fret buttons (0-9)
- ✅ Technique symbols (PM, bend, slide, hammer-on, pull-off, vibrato)
- ✅ Copy/paste measures (Ctrl+C/V)
- ✅ Delete/backspace to clear notes
- ✅ Interactive fretboard visualization
- ✅ Theory overlay on fretboard (scale notes, chord tones, root notes)
- ✅ Opacity control for theory guides (25%-100% "vapor wave")
- ✅ Quick chord inserter (from chord library)
- ✅ Note name tooltips
- ✅ Intelligence panel (theory suggestions)
- ✅ Metronome (BPM-based click)

### **5. Drum Editor**
- ✅ Dedicated 4-line drum staff (C/R, HH, SD, BD)
- ✅ Proper drum notation (x, o, X symbols)
- ✅ Keyboard shortcuts (X, O, C keys)
- ✅ Arrow navigation
- ✅ Quick insert buttons (Kick, Snare, Hi-Hat, Crash)
- ✅ Pattern library integration
- ✅ NOT a guitar hack - proper drum engine

### **6. Vocal Editor**

- ✅ Lyric entry (one syllable per beat)
- ✅ Melody notation (C3-B5 range)
- ✅ Quick note buttons (C4-C5)
- ✅ Phrasing marks (breath, phrase end, hold)
- ✅ Performance notes (free text)
- ✅ Vocal technique tracking (vibrato, falsetto, belt, breathy, growl)
- ✅ Dynamics selector (pp, p, mp, mf, f, ff)
- ✅ Harmony settings (UI exists)
- ✅ Vocal range helper (shows ranges)
- ✅ Export lyrics to .txt file

### **7. Theory Sandbox**

- ✅ Scale explorer (all modes: Major, Minor, Dorian, Phrygian, etc.)
- ✅ Chord builder with analysis
- ✅ Progression analyzer
- ✅ Interactive fretboard visualization
- ✅ Diatonic chord display
- ✅ Theory output panel (detailed analysis)

### **8. AI & Intelligence**

- ✅ Scale-aware suggestions
